|\Theta|=|\Theta_{1}|+|\Theta_{2}|+1\leq|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|+1=|\Phi|+|\Psi|
.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

t=\lambda y.{u}
.
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\sf fv}(v_{\mathsf{t}})\cup\{x\}
, hence
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=\lambda y.{u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}}
and
\Phi
is necessarily of the form (for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
)

\mathsf{many}

\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.25005pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 41.95377pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{u}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}
{\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i};x\!:\!\uplus_{i=1}^{n}{\mathsf{N}}_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{u}\!:\!\uplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}_{i}]

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)
and
|\Phi|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|+1)
.
Since
y\notin{\sf fv}(v_{\mathsf{t}})
, then
y\notin\mathsf{dom}(\Delta)
(Remark
D.1
), and so

\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta,y\!:\!\mathbf{0}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
.
Now, there are two subcases:

–

Empty multi type
: If
n=0
, then
{\mathsf{N}}=\mathbf{0}={\mathsf{M}}
and
\mathsf{dom}(\Gamma)=\emptyset
, with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Phi|
.
According to Lemma
D.1
.
1
applied to
\Psi
,
\mathsf{dom}(\Delta)=\emptyset
with

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Psi|
.
We can then build the derivation

\Theta=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.92595pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${}\vdash\lambda y.(u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\})\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta|=0=|\Phi|+|\Psi|
, and
\Theta\vartriangleright\Gamma\uplus\Delta\vdash t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
since
\mathsf{dom}(\Gamma\uplus\Delta)=\emptyset
.

–

Non-empty multi type
: If
n>0
, then we can decompose
\Psi
according to the partitioning

{\mathsf{N}}=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
by repeatedly applying Lemma
D.1
.
2
, and hence
for all
1\leq i\leq n
there are context
\Delta_{i}
and a derivation

\Psi_{i}\vartriangleright{\Delta_{i};y\!:\!\mathbf{0}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
such that

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|
.
By
i.h.
, for all
1\leq i\leq n
, there is a derivation

\Theta_{i}\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{i}\uplus\Delta_{i},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}
such that
|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and

|\Theta_{i}|\leq|\Phi_{i}|+|\Psi_{i}|
.
Since
\Gamma\uplus\Delta=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}(\Gamma_{i}\uplus\Delta_{i})
, we can build

\Theta
as

\mathsf{many}

\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 10.13895pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 58.22342pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\uplus\Delta_{i},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{i}\uplus\Delta_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{(u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\})}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}
\Gamma\uplus\Delta\vdash\lambda y.{(u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\})}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

noting that
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and that
|\Theta|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Theta_{i}|+1)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|+|\Psi_{i}|+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Phi_{i}|+1)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|=|\Phi|+|\Psi|
.

•

Explicit substitution
,
i.e.

t=u[y{\shortleftarrow}s]
.
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\sf fv}(v_{\mathsf{t}})\cup\{x\}
, hence
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}[y{\shortleftarrow}s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}]
and necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\thinspace\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.92363pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.78133pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{2},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash u[y{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
,
|\Phi|=|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|+1
,
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}
and
{\mathsf{N}}={\mathsf{N}}_{2}\uplus{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
.
According to Lemma
D.1
.
2
applied to
\Psi
and to the decomposition
{\mathsf{N}}={\mathsf{N}}_{1}\uplus{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
, there are contexts
\Delta_{1},\Delta_{2}
and derivations

\Psi_{1}\vartriangleright{\Delta_{1}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}
and

\Psi_{2}\vartriangleright{\Delta_{2}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
such that
\Delta=\Delta_{1}\uplus\Delta_{2}
,
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|=|\Psi_{1}|+|\Psi_{2}|
.

By
i.h.
, there are derivations
\Theta_{1}\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Delta_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and
\Theta_{2}\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{2}\uplus\Delta_{2}}\vdash s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
such that
|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta_{i}|\leq|\Phi_{i}|+|\Psi_{i}|
for all
i\in\{1,2\}
.
Since
\Gamma\uplus\Delta=\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Delta_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}\uplus\Delta_{2}
, we can
build the derivation

\Theta=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.4537pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 45.1933pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 56.05098pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Delta_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{2}\uplus\Delta_{2}\vdash s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma\uplus\Delta\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}[y{\shortleftarrow}s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

verifying that
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and

|\Theta|=1+|\Theta_{1}|+|\Theta_{2}|\leq 1+(|\Phi_{1}|+|\Psi_{1}|)+(|\Phi_{2}|+|\Psi_{2}|)=|\Phi|+|\Psi|
.
∎

Lemma D.3

(Typing of values: merging)
.

Let
v_{\mathsf{t}}
be a theoretical value.

1.

There is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright{}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\mathbf{0}
with

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Phi|
.

2.

For every derivations

\Phi_{1}\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{1}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{1}
and

\Phi_{2}\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{2}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{2}
, there exists a derivation

\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{1}\uplus{\mathsf{M}}_{2}
such that
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|
.

Proof.

1.

Let
\Phi
be the following type derivation (applying the rule
\mathsf{many}
with
n=0
) such that

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Phi|
:

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 12.54741pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{${}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\ .

2.

Let

\Phi_{1}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.08417pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=27.69427pt,depth=1.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.46007pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{i}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{{i\in I}}$}}}\hbox{${\biguplus_{{i\in I}}\Gamma_{i}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\biguplus_{{i\in I}}[{\mathsf{A}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma_{1}=\biguplus_{{i\in I}}\Gamma_{i}
and
{\mathsf{M}}_{1}=\biguplus_{{i\in I}}[{\mathsf{A}}_{i}]
, and let

\Phi_{2}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\qquad\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=29.05537pt,depth=2.8611pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 20.34435pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{j}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{j}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{j}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{{j\in J}}$}}}\hbox{${\biguplus_{{j\in J}}\Gamma_{j}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\biguplus_{{j\in J}}[{\mathsf{A}}_{j}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma_{2}=\biguplus_{{j\in J}}\Gamma_{j}
and
{\mathsf{M}}_{2}=\biguplus_{{j\in J}}[{\mathsf{A}}_{j}]
.

We can derive
\Phi
by setting
K=I\cup J
and then

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.7966pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=27.69427pt,depth=1.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.05095pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{k}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{k}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{k}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{{k\in K}}$}}}\hbox{${\biguplus_{{k\in K}}\Gamma_{k}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\biguplus_{{k\in K}}[{\mathsf{A}}_{k}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

trivially verifying the statement.
∎

Lemma D.4

(Anti-substitution)
.
†
†
margin:

Proof p.
IV.3

Let
t
be a term,
v_{\mathsf{t}}
be a theoretical value, and

\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

be a type derivation. Then there are two derivations
\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

and
\Theta\vartriangleright{\Sigma}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|\leq|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

Proof.

By induction on the term
t
.
Cases:

•

Variable
, then are two sub-cases (let
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
):

1.

t=x
, then
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=v_{\mathsf{t}}
.
Let
\Sigma=\Gamma
, let
\Delta
be the empty context (
i.e.

\mathsf{dom}(\Delta)=\emptyset
), let

{\mathsf{N}}={\mathsf{M}}
and let
\Psi
be the derivation

\Psi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 13.65279pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.26973pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.31253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Thus,
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
and

|\Psi|=n
.
Let
\Theta=\Phi
: so,
\Theta\vartriangleright{\Sigma}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
and
\Sigma\uplus\Delta=\Gamma
with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}

and
|\Phi|=|\Theta|\leq|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

2.

t=z\neq x
, then
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=z
and
the derivation
\Phi
has necessarily the form (for some
n~\in~\mathbb{N}
)

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 13.65279pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.64476pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 25.68756pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${z\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]}\vdash z\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{${z\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash z\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${z\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash z\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
and
\Gamma=z\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
(while
\Gamma(x)=\mathbf{0}
).
Thus,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
and
|\Phi|=n
.
Let
\Sigma
be the empty context (
i.e.

\mathsf{dom}(\Sigma)=0
) and
{\mathsf{N}}=\mathbf{0}
.
By Lemma
D.3
.
1
, there is a derivation

\Theta\vartriangleright{}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\mathbf{0}
(and hence

\Theta\vartriangleright{\Sigma}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
) such that
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Theta|
.
Let
\Delta=\Gamma
and
\Psi=\Phi
:
therefore,
\Sigma\uplus\Delta=\Gamma

and
\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=|\Psi|\leq|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

•

Application
,
i.e.

t=t_{1}t_{2}
.
Then
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=t_{1}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}t_{2}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}
and necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 13.36342pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.62733pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 48.33572pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{1}\vdash t_{1}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{2}\vdash t_{2}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash t_{1}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}t_{2}\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
,
|\Phi|=|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|+1
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}
. By
i.h.
, for all
i\in\{1,2\}
, there are derivations
\Psi_{i}\vartriangleright{\Delta_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}}\vdash t_{i}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
and
\Theta_{i}\vartriangleright{\Sigma_{i}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
such that
\Gamma_{i}=\Delta_{i}\uplus\Sigma_{i}
with
|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi_{i}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|+|\Theta_{i}|
.
According to Lemma
D.3
.
2
applied to
\Theta_{1}
and
\Theta_{2}
, there is a
derivation
\Theta\vartriangleright{\Sigma}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
where
{\mathsf{N}}={\mathsf{N}}_{1}\uplus{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
and
\Sigma=\Sigma_{1}\uplus\Sigma_{2}
, such that
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|=|\Psi_{1}|+|\Psi_{2}|
.
We can build the derivation (where
\Delta=\Delta_{1}\uplus\Delta_{2}
)

\Psi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hskip 13.53473pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.67018pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 44.37856pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash t_{1}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{2},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}\vdash t_{2}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|+1=|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}

and

|\Phi|=|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|+1\leq|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|+1=|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

t=\lambda y.{u}
.
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\sf fv}(v_{\mathsf{t}})\cup\{x\}
, hence
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=\lambda y.{u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}}
and necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.49542pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\quad\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.82846pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 47.53102pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$$}}}}\hbox{${\Gamma}\vdash\lambda y.{u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}
,
{\mathsf{M}}=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}]
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and

|\Phi|=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|
.

\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta,y\!:\!\mathbf{0}}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
.
There are two subcases:

–

Empty multi type
: If
n=0
, then
{\mathsf{M}}=\mathbf{0}
and
\mathsf{dom}(\Gamma)=\emptyset
, with

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Phi|
.
We can build the derivation

\Psi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.0625pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${}\vdash\lambda y.u\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Psi|
.
Let
{\mathsf{N}}=\mathbf{0}
and
\Delta
be the empty context (
i.e.

\mathsf{dom}(\Delta)=\emptyset
): then

\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
. According to Lemma
D.3
.
1
, there is a derivation

\Theta\vartriangleright\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!\mathbf{0}
with
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Theta|
.
Let
\Sigma
be the empty context (
i.e.

\mathsf{dom}(\Sigma)=\emptyset
): so,

\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=0\leq|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

–

Non-empty multi type
: If
n>0
then by
i.h.
, for all
1\leq i\leq n
, there are derivations

\Psi_{i}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}
and

\Theta_{i}\vartriangleright\Sigma_{i}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
such that
\Gamma_{i}=\Delta_{i}\uplus\Sigma_{i}

with
|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi_{i}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|+|\Theta_{i}|
.
We can build the derivation

\Psi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 30.32034pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.25005pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 42.99544pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i};y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i};x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta_{i};x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{u}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{${\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Delta_{i};x\!:\!\uplus_{i=1}^{n}{\mathsf{N}}_{i}}\vdash\lambda y.{u}\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\!\multimap\!{\mathsf{M}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Thus,
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|
.
By repeatedly applying Lemma
D.3
.
2
, there is a derivation

\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with
\Sigma=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Sigma_{i}
such that

|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Theta_{i}|
.
So,
\Gamma=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}(\Delta_{i}\uplus\Sigma_{i})=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}})=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}

and
|\Phi|=n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|\leq n+\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|+|\Theta_{i}|)=|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.

•

Explicit substitution
,
i.e.

t=u[y{\shortleftarrow}s]
.
We can suppose without loss of generality that
y\notin{\sf fv}(v_{\mathsf{t}})\cup\{x\}
, hence
t\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}=u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}[y{\shortleftarrow}s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}]
and necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.58568pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 33.92247pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 44.78015pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{2}\vdash s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}[y{\shortleftarrow}s\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
,
|\Phi|=|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|+1
and
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Phi_{1}
and Remark
D.1
, there are derivations

\Psi_{1}\vartriangleright\Delta_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and

\Theta_{1}\vartriangleright\Sigma_{1}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}
with
\Gamma_{1}=\Delta_{1}\uplus\Sigma_{1}

such that
|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi_{1}|\leq|\Psi_{1}|+|\Theta_{1}|
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Phi_{2}
, there are derivations
\Psi_{2}\vartriangleright\Delta_{2},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and

\Theta_{2}\vartriangleright\Sigma_{2}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
with
\Gamma_{2}=\Delta_{2}\uplus\Sigma_{2}

such that
|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi_{2}|\leq|\Psi_{2}|+|\Theta_{2}|
.
According to Lemma
D.3
.
2
, there is a derivation

\Theta\vartriangleright{\Sigma}\vdash v_{\mathsf{t}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with
\Sigma=\Sigma_{1}\uplus\Sigma_{2}
and
{\mathsf{N}}={\mathsf{N}}_{1}\uplus{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
such that
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta|=|\Theta_{1}|+|\Theta_{2}|
.
We can build the derivation (where
\Delta=\Delta_{1}\uplus\Delta_{2}
)

\Psi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\thinspace\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.9653pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 40.823pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},y\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{2}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{2},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash u[y{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

verifying that
\Gamma=\Gamma_{1}\uplus\Gamma_{2}=\Delta_{1}\uplus\Sigma_{1}\uplus\Delta_{2}\uplus\Sigma_{2}=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{1}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta_{2}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Phi_{1}|+|\Phi_{2}|\leq 1+(|\Psi_{1}|+|\Theta_{1}|)+(|\Theta_{2}|+|\Theta_{2}|)=|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.
∎

Proposition D.5

(Qualitative subjects)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
IV.4

proposition

IV.4

Let
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}t^{\prime}
.

1.

Reduction
: if
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
then there is a derivation
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|\leq|\Phi|
.

2.

Expansion
: if
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|\leq|\Phi|
.

Proof.

1.

By induction on the evaluation context
C
in the step
t=C\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}C\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
or
u^{\prime}\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.
The proof is analogous to the one for open quantitative subject reduction (
Proposition

V.3
), except that now there is one more case:

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

C=\lambda x.{C^{\prime}}
.
Then,
t=C\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle}\allowbreak\mapsto_{a}\lambda x.{C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=C\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Then, the derivation
\Phi
is necessarily (for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
)

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.78271pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.75131pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

By
i.h.
, for all
1\leq i\leq n
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{i}^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
with:

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|
and
|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.80649pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.7827pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 41.1541pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.35107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

(a)

|\Phi^{\prime}|=1+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|\leq 1+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|=|\Phi|
(note that
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|
if
n=0
);

(b)

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
(note that
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
n=0
).

2.

By induction on the evaluation context
C
in the step
t=C\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}C\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
or
u^{\prime}\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.
The proof is analogous to the one for open quantitative subject expansion (
Proposition

V.6
), except that now there is one more case:

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

C=\lambda x.{C^{\prime}}
.
Then,
t=C\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle}\allowbreak\mapsto_{a}\lambda x.{C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=C\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Then, the derivation
\Phi^{\prime}
is necessarily (for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
)

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.80649pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.7827pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 41.1541pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.35107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

By
i.h.
, for all
1\leq i\leq n
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{i}\vartriangleright\Gamma_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
with

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|
and

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.78271pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.75131pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.C^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

(a)

|\Phi|=1+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|\leq 1+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|=|\Phi^{\prime}|
(note that
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|
if
n=0
);

(b)

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
(note that
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
n=0
).
∎

Proposition D.6

(Invariance under evaluation)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
IV.5

proposition

IV.5

Let
\vec{x}
be suitable for
t
.
If
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}^{*}t^{\prime}
then
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}=\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}
.

Proof.

It is enough to show that
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}t^{\prime}
implies
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}=\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}
.
Let
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}t^{\prime}
.
For any
\Phi\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
, there is
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
by subject reduction (
Proposition

IV.4
.
1
), so
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}\subseteq\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}
.
Conversely, for any
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
, there is
\Phi\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
by subject expansion (
Proposition

IV.4
.
2
), so
\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}\subseteq\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}
.
∎

Appendix E
Proofs of
Section

V

Remark E.1

(Merging and splitting inertness)
.

Let
{\mathsf{M}},{\mathsf{N}},{\mathsf{O}}
be multi types with
{\mathsf{M}}={\mathsf{N}}\uplus{\mathsf{O}}
; then,
{\mathsf{M}}
is inert iff
{\mathsf{N}}
and
{\mathsf{O}}
are inert.
Similarly for type contexts.

Lemma E.1

(Spreading of inertness on judgments)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.1

lemma

V.1

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation and
i
be an inert term.
If
\Gamma
is a inert type context, then
{\mathsf{M}}
is a inert multi type.

Proof.

By induction on the definition of inert terms
i
.
Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

i=x
. Then necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 26.35654pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.26973pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.31253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${\Gamma}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
and
\Gamma=x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.
As
\Gamma
is inert (by hypothesis), so is
{\mathsf{M}}
.

•

Application
,
i.e.

f=if^{\prime}
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 3.80588pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.4861pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.08371pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash i\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash f^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash if^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
As
\Gamma
is inert, so is
\Delta
, according to
Remark

E.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Psi
,
[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
is inert and hence
{\mathsf{M}}
is inert.

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

i=i^{\prime\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.99687pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.22253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.10802pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash i^{\prime\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash i^{\prime\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
As
\Gamma
is inert, so are
\Delta
and
\Sigma
, according to
Remark

E.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Theta
,
{\mathsf{N}}
is inert.
Therefore,
\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
is a inert type context.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Psi
, the multi type
{\mathsf{M}}
is inert.
∎

Correctness

Lemma E.2

(Size of fireballs)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.2

lemma

V.2

Let
f
be a fireball.
If
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash f\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
If, moreover,
\Gamma
is inert and (
{\mathsf{M}}
is ground inert or
f
is inert), then

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.

Proof.

By mutual induction on the definition of fireballs
f
and inert terms
i
.
Cases for inert terms:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

f=x
. Then necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 26.35654pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.26973pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.31253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${\Gamma}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
and
\Gamma=x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=0=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

•

Application
,
i.e.

f=if^{\prime}
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 3.80588pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.4861pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.08371pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash i\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash f^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash if^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
By
i.h.
applied to both premises,
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|i|
and
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|f^{\prime}|
.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+1\leq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

If, moreover,
\Gamma
is inert then so are
\Delta
and
\Sigma
, according to

Remark

E.1
.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
\Psi
,
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
By spreading of inertness (
Lemma

V.1
),
[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
is inert, hence
{\mathsf{N}}=\mathbf{0}
.
By
i.h.
for fireballs applied to
\Theta
,
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
Therefore,
|f|=|i|+|f^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

f=i[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 14.44131pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.22253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 25.55246pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash i[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
We can then apply
i.h.
to both premises:
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
and
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|i^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
Therefore,
|f_{\mathsf{s}}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|i_{\mathsf{s}}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+|i^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}\leq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

If, moreover,
\Gamma
is inert then so are
\Delta
and
\Sigma
, according to

Remark

E.1
.
By spreading of inertness (
Lemma

V.1
),
{\mathsf{N}}
is inert, hence
\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
is a inert type context.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
\Psi
and
\Theta
, we have
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
and
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|i^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
So,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+|i^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

Cases for fireballs that are not inert terms:

•

Explicit substitution on fireball
,
i.e.

f=f^{\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}i]
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.10767pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.81976pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.2188pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash f^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash f^{\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}i]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
We can then apply
i.h.
to both premises:
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
and
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+|i_{\mathsf{s}}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}\leq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}

If, moreover,
\Gamma
is inert and
{\mathsf{M}}
is ground inert, then
\Delta
and
\Sigma
are inert, according to
Remark

E.1
.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
\Theta
,
|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
By spreading of inertness (
Lemma

V.1
),
{\mathsf{N}}
is inert, hence
\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
is a inert type context.
By
i.h.
for fireballs applied to
\Psi
,
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}
.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|f^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+|i|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

f=\lambda x.{t}
.
Then necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 26.5678pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\quad\hbox{\hbox{}\quad\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.52782pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 32.0643pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.52782pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 30.62851pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{1},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{1}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{1}}\vdash\lambda x.{t}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{n}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{n},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{n}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Gamma_{n}}\vdash\lambda x.{t}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{n}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${\Gamma}\vdash\lambda x.{t}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]
and
\Gamma=\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}
.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=0\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

If, moreover,
{\mathsf{M}}
is ground inert, then necessarily
{\mathsf{M}}=\mathbf{0}
and
n=0
, hence
\Phi
consist of the rule
\mathsf{many}
with
0
premises.
Therefore,
|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=0=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
∎

Proposition E.3

(Open quantitative subject reduction)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.3

proposition

V.3

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation.

1.

If
t\rightarrow_{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
then there exists a derivation
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
;

2.

If
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
then there exists a derivation
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Proof.

By induction on the open evaluation context
O
such that
t=O\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
or
u^{\prime}\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.
Cases for
O
:

•

Hole
,
i.e.

O=\langle\cdot\rangle
.
Then there are two sub-cases:

1.

Multiplicative
,
i.e.

t=L\langle\lambda x.u\rangle s\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}L\langle u[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\rangle=t^{\prime}
.
Then
\Phi
has necessarily the form:

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 23.081pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower x.u\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip with

.
We can then build
\Phi^{\prime}
as follows:

.

2.

Exponential
,
i.e.

.

\Phi
has necessarily the form:

to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{n}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\quad$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and

.
By the substitution lemma (Lemma
IV.2
), there is a derivation
\Phi^{\prime\prime}\vartriangleright{\Delta\uplus\Sigma^{\prime}}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

.

:
