3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 44.92014pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi^{\prime\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma^{\prime}\vdash u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v_{\mathsf{t}}\}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{1}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\quad$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$$}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to10.33325pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\par}}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{n}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\quad$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash L\langle u\{x{\shortleftarrow}v\}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=n+|\Phi^{\prime\prime}|+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|\leq n+|\Psi|+|\Theta|+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|<1+n+|\Psi|+|\Theta|+\sum_{i=1}^{n}|\Phi_{i}|=|\Phi|
(
\Phi^{\prime}
contains at least one rule
\mathsf{es}
less than
\Phi
).

•

Application left
,
i.e.

O=O^{\prime}s
.
Then,
t=O\langle u\rangle=O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle s\mapsto_{a}O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle s=O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
The derivation
\Phi
is necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.57892pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.07903pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
with:

1.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|-1
if
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|<|\Psi|
if
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.9817pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.48181pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

noting that

1.

If
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+(|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2)+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=1+(|\Psi|-1)+|\Theta|=|\Phi|-1
;

2.

If
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|<1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|=|\Phi|
.

•

Application right
,
i.e.

O=sO^{\prime}
.
Analogous to the previous case.

•

Explicit substitution left
,
i.e.

O=O^{\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}s]
.
Then,
t=O\langle u\rangle=O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\mapsto_{a}O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]=O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
The derivation
\Phi
is necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.21436pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.93663pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta;x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with:

1.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|-1
if
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|<|\Psi|
if
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.00598pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.33942pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta;x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

noting that

1.

If
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=(|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2)+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=(|\Psi|-1)+|\Theta|=|\Phi|-1
;

2.

If
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|<|\Psi|+|\Theta|=|\Phi|
.

•

Explicit substitution right
,
i.e.

O=s[x{\shortleftarrow}O^{\prime}]
.
Analogous to the previous case.
∎

Theorem E.4

(Open correctness)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.4

theorem

V.4

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright t
be a derivation.
Then there is an
{\mathsf{o}}
-normalizing evaluation
d\colon t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}^{*}u
with
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|u|_{{\mathsf{o}}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
And if
\Phi
is tight, then
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

Proof.

Given the derivation (resp. tight derivation)
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
, we proceed by induction on the general size
|\Phi|
of
\Phi
.

If
t
is normal for
\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}
, then
t=f
is a fireball.
Let
d
be the empty evaluation (so
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
), thus
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}

(resp.
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
) by Lemma
V.2
.

Otherwise,
t
is not normal for
\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}
and so
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}u
.
According to open subject reduction (
Proposition

V.3
), there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that
|\Psi|<|\Phi|
and

•

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
(resp.
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
) if
t\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u
,

•

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u
.

By
i.h.
, there exists a fireball
f
and a reduction sequence
d^{\prime}\colon u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}^{*}f
with

2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}\leq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
(resp.
2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
).
Let
d
be the
{\mathsf{o}}
-evaluation obtained by concatenating the first step
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}u
and
d^{\prime}
.
There are two cases:

•

Multiplicative:
if
t\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2\geq|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
(resp.
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|
), since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
.

•

Exponential:
if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
(resp.
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
), since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
∎

Completeness

Proposition E.5

(Tight typability of open normal forms)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.5

lemma

V.5

1.

Inert:
if
t
is an inert term then, for any inert multi type
{\mathsf{M}}
, there is an inert
type derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.

2.

Fireball:
if
t
is a fireball then there is a tight derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!\mathbf{0}
.

Proof.

We prove simultaneously
Items

2
and
1
by
mutual induction on the definition of fireball and inert term.
Note that
Item

1
is stronger than
Item

2
.
Cases for inert terms:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

t=x
, which is an inert term.
Let
{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}
be an inert multi type: hence,
{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
and some
{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}
inert linear types.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.65279pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\quad\hbox{\hbox{}\quad\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.65858pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.70139pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\dots$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]\vdash x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
is an inert type context.

•

Inert application
,
i.e.

t=if
for some inert term
i
and fireball
f
.
Let
{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}
be a multi type.
By
i.h.
for fireballs, there is a derivation
\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash f\!:\!\mathbf{0}
for some inert type context
\Sigma
.
By
i.h.
for inert terms, since
[\mathbf{0}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}]
is an inert multi type, there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta\vdash i\!:\![\mathbf{0}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}]
for some inert type context
\Delta
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 6.94756pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.41663pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.50311pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash i\!:\![\mathbf{0}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash f\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash if\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
is an inert type context, by
Remark

E.1
.

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

t=i[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]
for some inert terms
i
and
i^{\prime}
.
Let
{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}
be an inert multi type.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
i
, there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}
for some inert multi type
{\mathsf{N}}
and inert type context
\Delta
.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
i^{\prime}
, there is a derivation
\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash i^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
for some inert type context
\Sigma
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.91628pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.22253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.63857pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash i[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\mathsf{i}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
is an inert type context, by
Remark

E.1
.

Cases for fireballs that may not be inert terms:

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

t=\lambda x.{u}
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.45602pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$\vdash\lambda x.{u}\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where the type context
\Gamma
is empty and hence inert, thus
\Phi
is tight.

•

Explicit substitution on fireball
,
i.e.

t=f[x{\shortleftarrow}i]
for some fireball
f
and inert term
i
.
By
i.h.
for fireballs applied to
f
, there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\mathsf{i}}\vdash f\!:\!\mathbf{0}
for some inert multi type
{\mathsf{N}}^{\mathsf{i}}
and inert type context
\Delta
.
By
i.h.
for inert terms applied to
i
, there is a derivation
\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\mathsf{i}}
for some inert type context
\Sigma
.
We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 14.50758pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.90587pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.4021pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\mathsf{i}}\vdash f\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\mathsf{i}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash f[x{\shortleftarrow}i]\!:\!\mathbf{0}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
is an inert type context, by
Remark

E.1
.
Therefore,
\Phi
is tight.
∎

Proposition E.6

(Open quantitative subject expansion)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.6

proposition

V.6

Let
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation.

1.

Multiplicative step:
if
t\rightarrow_{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
;

2.

Exponential step:
if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Proof.

By induction on the evaluation context
O
in the step
t=O\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
or
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.
Cases for
O
:

•

Hole
,
i.e.

O=\langle\cdot\rangle
.
Then there are two sub-cases:

1.

Multiplicative
,
i.e.

t=L\langle\lambda x.u\rangle s\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}L\langle u[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\rangle=t^{\prime}
.
Then
\Phi^{\prime}
has necessarily the form:

•

Application left
,
i.e.

O=O^{\prime}s
.
Then,
t=O\langle u\rangle=O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle s\mapsto_{a}O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle s=O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
The derivation
\Phi^{\prime}
is necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.9817pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.48181pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
,
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
with:

1.

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2
and
|\Psi|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1
if
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|<|\Psi^{\prime}|
if
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.57892pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.07903pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

noting that

1.

If
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+(|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2)+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|=1+1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=|\Phi^{\prime}|+1
;

2.

If
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=1+|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|>1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=|\Phi^{\prime}|
.

•

Application right
,
i.e.

O=sO^{\prime}
.
Analogous to the previous case.

•

Explicit substitution left
,
i.e.

O=O^{\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}s]
.
Then,
t=O\langle u\rangle=O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\mapsto_{a}O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]=O\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\mapsto_{a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
The derivation
\Phi^{\prime}
is necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.00598pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 37.33942pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta;x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u^{\prime}\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright{\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with:

1.

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2
and
|\Psi|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1
if
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi|>|\Psi^{\prime}|
if
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.21436pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.0521pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.93663pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Delta;x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{${\Sigma}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash O^{\prime}\langle u\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}s]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

noting that

1.

If
u\mapsto_{\mathsf{m}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=(|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2)+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2
and
|\Phi|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|=1+1+|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=|\Phi^{\prime}|
;

2.

If
u\mapsto_{{\mathsf{e}}}u^{\prime}
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\rho|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi|=|\Psi|+|\Theta|>|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=|\Phi^{\prime}|
.

•

Explicit substitution right
,
i.e.

O=s[x{\shortleftarrow}O^{\prime}]
.
Analogous to the previous case.
∎

Theorem E.7

(Open completeness)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.7

theorem

V.7

Let
d\colon t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}^{*}u
be an
{\mathsf{o}}
-normalizing evaluation.
Then there is a tight derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!\mathbf{0}
such that
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|f|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the length
|d|
of the
{\mathsf{o}}
-evaluation
d
.

If
|d|=0
then
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
and
t=f
is
{\mathsf{o}}
-normal and hence a fireball.
According to tight typability of fireballs (
Lemma

V.5
), there is a tight derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!\mathbf{0}
.
Therefore,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
by
Lemma

V.2
.

Otherwise,
|d|>0
and
d
is the concatenation of a first step
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}u
and an evaluation
d^{\prime}\colon u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}^{*}f
, with
|d|=1+|d^{\prime}|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a tight derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash u\!:\!\mathbf{0}
such that
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
According to open subject expansion (
Proposition

V.6
), there is a derivation

\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!\mathbf{0}
with

•

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}

if
t\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u
, since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
;

•

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|f|_{{\mathsf{o}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u
, since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
∎

Theorem E.8

(Open adequacy)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
V.8

theorem

V.8

Let
t
be a term, with
\vec{x}
suitable for
t
.
Then,
t
is
{\mathsf{o}}
-normalizing if and only if
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
.

Proof.

According to open completeness (
Theorem

V.7
), if
t
is
{\mathsf{o}}
-normalizing, then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
for some type context
\Gamma
and multi type
{\mathsf{M}}
, thus
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
.

Conversely, if
\llbracket t\rrbracket_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
, then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
for some type context
\Gamma
and multi type
{\mathsf{M}}
, thus by open correctness (
Theorem

V.4
),
t
is
{\mathsf{o}}
-normalizing.
∎

Appendix F
Proofs of
Section

VI

Remark

F.1
and
Lemma

VI.1
below are used to prove both correctness (
Theorem

VI.4
) and completeness (
Theorem

VI.7
)

Remark F.1

(Merging and splitting
left
shrinkingess)
.

Let
{\mathsf{M}},{\mathsf{N}},{\mathsf{O}}
be multi types with
{\mathsf{M}}={\mathsf{N}}\uplus{\mathsf{O}}
; then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) iff
{\mathsf{N}}
and
{\mathsf{O}}
are
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
).
Similarly for type contexts.

The property above fails for unitary
right
shrinkingness.
Indeed,
[{\mathsf{G}}]
is unitary
right
but
[{\mathsf{G}}]\uplus[{\mathsf{G}}]=[{\mathsf{G}},{\mathsf{G}}]
is
right
and unitary
left
, but not unitary
right
.

Lemma F.1

(Spreading of
left
shrinkingness)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.1

lemma

VI.1

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation and
r
a rigid term.
If
\Gamma
is a
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) type context, then
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) multi type.

Proof.

By induction on the definition of the rigid term
r
.
Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

r=x
.
Then necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 26.35654pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.65858pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize{$\mathsf{ax}$}}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.70139pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
and
\Gamma=x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.
Since
\Gamma
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) type context,
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) multi type.

•

Application
,
i.e.

r=r^{\prime}t
where
r^{\prime}
is a rigid term.
The derivation
\Phi
is necessarily (with
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
)

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.26964pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 15.90276pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.1586pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash r^{\prime}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r^{\prime}t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Delta
and
\Sigma
are
left
(resp. unitary
left
) type contexts by
Remark
F.1
.
By
i.h.
,
[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) multi type, and hence
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) multi type.

•

Explicit substitution
,
i.e.

r=r^{\prime}[x{\shortleftarrow}r^{\prime\prime}]
where
r^{\prime}
and
r^{\prime\prime}
are
rigid terms.
The derivation
\Phi
is necessarily (with
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
)

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.50229pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.04744pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.62735pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash r^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash r^{\prime\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r[x{\shortleftarrow}r^{\prime\prime}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Delta
and
\Sigma
are
left
(resp. unitary
left
) contexts by
Remark
F.1
.
By

applied to
\Theta
(as

is a rigid term),
{\mathsf{N}}
is a

(resp. unitary

) multi type, and hence

is a

(resp. unitary
left
) type context.
By

applied to
\Psi
(as

is a rigid term),
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left

∎

F-A

Correctness

We aim to prove correctness (
Theorem

VI.4
), refined with quantitative information:
if a term is shrinking typable then it terminates, and the type derivation provides bounds for both the number of
multiplicative steps to normal form and the size of the normal form.

The are two crucial ingredients:

1.

shrinking type derivations bound the size of normal forms (
Lemma

VI.2
), and unitary
shrinking type derivations give the exact size of normal forms;

2.

quantitative subject reduction (
Proposition

VI.3
) says that not only types are preserved
after a
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}
step but also that if the type derivation is shrinking then the size of the derivation strictly
decreases.

In both proofs
Lemma

VI.1
is used.

Lemma F.2

(Size of
strong
fireballs)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.2

lemma

VI.2

Let
\Gamma
be a
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) type context,
f_{\mathsf{s}}
be a
strong
fireball and
\Phi\vartriangleright{\Gamma}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation such that if
f_{\mathsf{s}}
is an
answer
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
right
(
resp.
unitary
right
).
Then
|f_{\mathsf{s}}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
(
resp.

|f_{\mathsf{s}}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
).

Proof.

By structural induction on
f_{\mathsf{s}}
.
Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

f_{\mathsf{s}}=x
. Then necessarily, for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 26.35654pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.26973pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.31253pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{${\Gamma}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

∎

We split the proof of shrinking quantitative subject reduction in two cases:

1.

Open:
after a
\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}
step any type derivation strictly shrinks its size
(
Proposition

V.3
);

2.

Strong:
after a
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}
step the derivation strictly shrinks its size if it is shrinking
(
Proposition

VI.3
).

More precisely, two sizes measure how much a derivation shrinks.
They play two different roles:

1.

Qualitative:
To have a combinatorial proof of the characterization of normalizable terms, we need a
measure
that strictly decreases for every (suitable) step to the normal form.
This role is played by the general size.

2.

Quantitative:
To measure the number of steps to reach the normal forms, we need a measure that decreases
by a constant number for each step. This is the role
played by the multiplicative size to count the number of multiplicative steps (it does not count the number of
exponential steps and, indeed, it does not change after an exponential step).

The open case (
Proposition

V.3
) represents the base case for the strong case
(
Proposition

VI.3
).
To prove the base case, we need a substitution lemma (
Lemma

IV.2
) to handle the exponential step, which in turn relies on
Lemma

D.1
.

Proposition F.3

(Shrinking quantitative subject reduction)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.3

proposition

VI.3

Let
\Gamma
be a
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) context.
Suppose that
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and if
t
is a
answer
, then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
right
(
resp.
unitary
right
).

1.

Multiplicative step:
If
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}\mathsf{m}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation

\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that
|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|

(
resp.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
);

2.

Exponential step:
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{e}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation

\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Proof.

First, we prove the unitary version of the statement (
i.e.
under the hypothesis that
\Gamma
is unitary
left
and if
t
is a
answer
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is unitary
right
).
The proof is by induction on the evaluation strong context
X
such that
t=X\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
or
u^{\prime}\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.
Cases for
X
:

•

Hole
,
i.e.

X=\langle\cdot\rangle
and
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}t^{\prime}
with
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
According to open subject reduction (
Proposition

V.3
),

–

if
t\rightarrow_{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
then there exists a derivation
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
;

–

if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and

|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Note that in this case the hypothesis that
\Gamma
is a (unitary)
left
context and that
{\mathsf{M}}
is a (unitary)
right
multi type if
t
is an
answer
are not used.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

X=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}}
.
So,
t=X\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle}\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Since
t
is an
answer
,
{\mathsf{M}}
is a unitary
right
multi type by hypothesis and hence it has the form

{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]
where
{\mathsf{O}}
is unitary
left
and
{\mathsf{N}}
is unitary
right
.
Thus, the derivation
\Phi
is necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 2.77779pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 8.74998pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 33.94913pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

By
i.h.
(as
\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
is a unitary
left
type context and
{\mathsf{N}}
is a unitary
right
multi type), there is a derivation
\Psi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with:

1.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|<|\Psi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 2.77779pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 8.74998pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.3519pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+1-2=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and

|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|+1-1=|\Phi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
);

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1<|\Psi|+1=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
).

•

Explicit substitution of rigid context
,
i.e.

X=s[x{\shortleftarrow}R]
.
So,
t=X\langle u\rangle=s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Then, necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.45135pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.68346pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.17365pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
unitary
left
by hypothesis, and then so is
\Sigma
by
Remark

F.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Theta
(as
R\langle u\rangle
is not an
answer
), there is a derivation

\Theta^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}

2.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|<|\Theta|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.45135pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.08624pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.17365pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2

|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|+|\Theta|-1+1=|\Phi|-1

u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.69388pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.49188pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 34.41615pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}r]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
is unitary
left
by hypothesis, and then so are
\Delta
and
\Sigma
by
Remark

F.1
.
According to spreading of
left
shrinkingness (
Lemma

VI.1
, as
r
is a rigid term),
{\mathsf{N}}
is unitary
left
.
Thus, the
i.h.
can be applied to
\Psi
(since
\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
is unitary
left
and
R\langle u\rangle
is not an
answer
) to obtain a derivation
\Psi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that:

1.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|<|\Psi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.09665pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.49188pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 35.81894pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle[x{\shortleftarrow}r]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|=|\Psi|-1+|\Theta|=|\Phi|-1
if

(
i.e.
if

);

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+|\Theta|<|\Psi|+|\Theta|=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
).

•

Rigid term with explicit substitution of rigid context
,
i.e.

X=r[x{\shortleftarrow}R]
.
Then,
t=X\langle u\rangle=r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
The derivation
\Phi
is necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.50229pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.68346pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.22458pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
unitary
left
by hypothesis, and then so is
\Sigma
by
Remark

F.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Theta
(as
R\langle u\rangle
is not an
answer
), there is a derivation

\Theta^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with:

1.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|=|\Theta|-1
if

u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|<|\Theta|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.50229pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.08624pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.22458pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|+|\Theta|-1+1=|\Phi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|+1<|\Psi|+|\Theta|+1=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

This completes the proof for the unitary case.

In the non-unitary statement (
i.e.
under the weaker hypothesis that
\Gamma
is a
left
type context and if
t
is an
answer
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
right
), the proof is analogous to the unitary statement, except for the
Abstraction
case.
Indeed, in the base case (
Hole context
) unitarity does not play any role, and the other cases follow from the
i.h.
analogously to the unitary statement.
Let us see the only substantially different case:

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

X=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}}
.
So,
t=X\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle}\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Since
t
is an
answer
,
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
right
multi type by hypothesis and hence it has the form
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{O}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{O}}_{n}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{n}]
for some
n>0
, where

{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
is
left
and
{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
is
right
for all
1\leq i\leq n
.
Thus, the derivation
\Phi
is necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.94305pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

For all
1\leq i\leq n
, by
i.h.
(as
\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
is a
left
type context and
{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
is a
right
multi type), there is a derivation
\Psi_{i}^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
with:

1.

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|<|\Psi_{i}|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.80649pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 40.34583pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.35107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1-2)=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2n\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
(where the last inequality holds because
n>0
) and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|+1-1)\leq|\Phi|-n<|\Phi|
(where the last inequality holds because
n>0
) if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|+1)<\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|+1)=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(the inequality holds because
n>0
).
∎

Theorem F.4

(Shrinking correctness)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.4

theorem

VI.4

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a shrinking (
resp.
unitary shrinking) derivation.
Then there are a
\mathsf{vsc}
-normal form
t^{\prime}
and an evaluation
d\colon t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}t^{\prime}
with
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
(
resp.

2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
).

Proof.

By induction on the general size
|\Phi|
of
\Phi
.

If
t
is
\mathsf{x}
-normal, then
t
is
\mathsf{vsc}
-normal by
Proposition

III.1
.
2
.
Let
t^{\prime}=t
and
d
be the empty evaluation (so
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
), thus
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}

(resp.
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
) by
Lemma

VI.2
.

Otherwise,
t
is not
\mathsf{x}
-normal and so
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}u
.
According to shrinking subject reduction (
Proposition

VI.3
), there is a derivation

\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that
|\Psi|<|\Phi|
and

•

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
(resp.
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
) if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{m}}}u
,

•

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u
.

By
i.h.
, there exists a normal form
t^{\prime}
and a reduction sequence
d^{\prime}\colon u\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}t^{\prime}
with
2|d^{\prime}|+|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}\leq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
(resp.
2|d^{\prime}|+|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
).
Let
d
be the
\mathsf{x}
-evaluation obtained by concatenating the first step
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}u
and
d^{\prime}
.
There are two cases:

•

Multiplicative:
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{m}}}u
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\geq|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2\geq|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
(resp.
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
), since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
.

•

Exponential:
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
, since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
∎

F-B

Completeness

The proof of completeness (
Theorem

VI.4
) is dual to the proof of (
Theorem

VI.4
), refined as usual
with quantitative information:
if a term noralizes then it is shrinking typable, and the derivation provides bounds for both the number of
multiplicative steps to normal form and the size of the normal form.

The are two crucial ingredients:

1.

normal forms are typable with a unitary shrinking type derivations (
Lemma

VI.5
);

2.

quantitative subject expansion (
Proposition

VI.6
) says that not only types are preserved
going back though a
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}
step but also that if the type derivation is shrinking then the size of the derivation
strictly increases.

Lemma F.5

(Shrinking typability of normal forms)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.5

lemma

VI.5

1.

Inert:

For every
strong
inert term
i_{\mathsf{s}}
and
left
multi type
{\mathsf{M}}
,
there exist a
left
type context
\Gamma

and a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.
If moreover
{\mathsf{M}}
is unitary, then so is
\Gamma
.

2.

Fireball:

For every
strong
fireball
f_{\mathsf{s}}

there exists a unitary shrinking derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.

Proof.

Both points are proved by mutual induction on the definition of
strong
inert terms
i_{\mathsf{s}}
and
strong
fireballs

f_{\mathsf{s}}
.
Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

i_{\mathsf{s}}=x=f_{\mathsf{s}}
.
Let
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]
be a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) multi type for some
n\in\mathbb{N}
.
We can build the following derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 22.52663pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.65858pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.70139pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{1}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{1}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\overset{n\in\mathbb{N}}{\ldots}$}}\hbox{}}\hbox{$x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{n}]\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{n}$}}\par}\par}}}}}\hbox{$x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
is a
left
(resp. unitary
left
) type context.
Moreover, if
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{G}}]
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is both a unitary
right
and a unitary
left
multi type, hence
\Gamma
is a unitary
left
type context and so also Point
2
holds.

•

Inert application
,
i.e.

.

\Sigma

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hskip 4.3941pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.23056pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 30.11981pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

,
