is a unitary
left
by

Remark

F.1
.
∎

As for quantitative subject reduction, we split the proof of quantitative subject expansion in two cases:

1.

Open:
going back though a
\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}}
step any type derivation strictly increases its size
(
Proposition

V.6
);

2.

Strong:
going back though a
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}
step the derivation strictly increases its size if it is
shrinking (
Proposition

VI.6
).

The open case (
Proposition

V.6
) represents the base case for the strong case
(
Proposition

VI.6
).
To prove the base case, we need an anti-substitution lemma (
Lemma

IV.3
) to handle the exponential step, which in turn relies on
Lemma

D.3
.

Proposition F.6

(Shrinking quantitative subject expansion)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.6

proposition

VI.6

Let
\Gamma
be a
left
(
resp.
unitary
left
) type context and
{\mathsf{M}}
be a multi type.
Suppose that
\Phi^{\prime}\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t^{\prime}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and if
t^{\prime}
is an
answer
, then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
right
(
resp.
unitary
right
).

1.

Multiplicative step:
If
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}\mathsf{m}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation

\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|

(
resp.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
);

2.

Exponential step:
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{e}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation

\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Proof.

First we prove the unitary version of the statement (
i.e.
under the hypothesis that
\Gamma
is
left
and if
t^{\prime}
is a
answer
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is unitary
right
).
The proof is by induction on the evaluation context
X
in the step
t=X\langle u\rangle\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with

u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
or
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.
Cases for
X
:

•

Hole context
,
i.e.

X=\langle\cdot\rangle
and
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}t^{\prime}
with
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
According to open subject expansion (
Proposition

V.6
),

–

if
t\rightarrow_{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Phi|-1
;

–

if
t\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
then there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|<|\Phi|
.

Note that in this case the hypotheses that
\Gamma
is unitary
left
and
{\mathsf{M}}
is unitary
right
if
t^{\prime}
is an
answer
are not used.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

X=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}}
.
Then,
t=X\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle}\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Since
t^{\prime}
is an
answer
,
{\mathsf{M}}
is a unitary
right
multi type by hypothesis and hence it has the form
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{2}]
where
{\mathsf{N}}_{1}
is unitary
left
and
{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
is unitary
right
.
Therefore, the derivation
\Phi^{\prime}
is necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 2.77779pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 8.74998pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 39.69916pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{2}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{2}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

By
i.h.
(as
\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}
is a unitary
left
type context and
{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
is a unitary
right
multi type), there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}
with:

1.

|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|
and
|\Psi^{\prime}|<|\Psi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 2.77779pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 8.74998pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.29639pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{2}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{2}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{2}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2+1=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and

|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|-1+1=|\Phi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
);

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi^{\prime}|+1<|\Psi|+1=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
).

•

Explicit substitution of rigid context
,
i.e.

X=s[x{\shortleftarrow}R]
.
So,
t=X\langle u\rangle=s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Then, necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.45135pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.08624pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.17365pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
, with
\Delta
and
\Sigma
unitary
left
by
Remark

F.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Theta^{\prime}
(as
R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle
is not a
answer
), there is a derivation
\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with:

1.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|=|\Theta|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Theta^{\prime}|=|\Theta|
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|<|\Theta|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.45135pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.68346pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.17365pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash s\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash s[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and

|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|+1=|\Psi|+|\Theta|-1+1=|\Phi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if

.
Thus, necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.50229pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.08624pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.22458pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.85107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u^{\prime}\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
, with
\Delta
and
\Sigma
unitary
left
type contexts by
Remark

F.1
.
By
i.h.
applied to
\Theta^{\prime}
(as
R\langle u\rangle
is not a
answer
), there is a derivation

\Theta\vartriangleright\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
with:

1.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|=|\Theta|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Theta^{\prime}|<|\Theta|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.50229pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.68346pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 26.22458pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash r\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to17.16656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Theta$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash R\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta\uplus\Sigma\vdash r[x{\shortleftarrow}R\langle u\rangle]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
and

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}-2=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|=1+|\Psi|+|\Theta|-1=|\Phi|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{m}}}t^{\prime}
);

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Theta|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=|\Psi|+|\Theta^{\prime}|+1<|\Psi|+|\Theta|+1=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(
i.e.
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{{\mathsf{e}}}}t^{\prime}
).

This completes the proof for the unitary case.

In the non-unitary statement (
i.e.
under the weaker hypothesis that
\Gamma
is a
left
type context and if
t^{\prime}
is an
answer
then
{\mathsf{M}}
is
right
), the proof is analogous to the unitary statement, except for the
Abstraction
case.
Indeed, in the base case (
Hole context
) unitarity does not play any role, and the other cases follow from the
i.h.
analogously to the unitary statement.
Let us see the only substantially different case:

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

X=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}}
.
So,
t=X\langle u\rangle=\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle}\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}a}\lambda x.{X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle}=X\langle u^{\prime}\rangle=t^{\prime}
with
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}a}u^{\prime}
and
a\in\{\mathsf{m},{\mathsf{e}}\}
.
Since
t^{\prime}
is an
answer
,
{\mathsf{M}}
is a
right
multi type by hypothesis and hence it has the form
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{O}}_{1}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{O}}_{n}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{n}]
for some
n>0
, where

{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
is
left
and
{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
is
right
for all
1\leq i\leq n
.
Thus, the derivation
\Phi^{\prime}
is necessarily

\Phi^{\prime}=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.80649pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 38.94305pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.35107pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}^{\prime}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

For all
1\leq i\leq n
, by
i.h.
(as
\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
is a
left
type context and
{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
is a
right
multi type), there is a derivation
\Psi_{i}\vartriangleright\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}
with:

1.

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
and
|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|\leq|\Psi_{i}|-1
if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|<|\Psi_{i}|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
.

We can then build the derivation

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.7321pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left(\vrule height=36.12198pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 40.34583pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right)_{1\leq i\leq n}$}}}\hbox{$\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.X^{\prime}\!\langle u^{\prime}\rangle\!:\!\biguplus_{i=1}^{n}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where

1.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)\leq\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1-2)=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2n\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}-2
(where the last inequality holds because
n>0
) and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|+1-1)\leq|\Phi|-n<|\Phi|
(where the last inequality holds because
n>0
) if
u\rightarrow_{{{{\mathsf{o}}\mathsf{m}}}}u^{\prime}
;

2.

|\Phi^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
|\Phi^{\prime}|=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}^{\prime}|+1)<\sum_{i=1}^{n}(|\Psi_{i}|+1)=|\Phi|
if
u\rightarrow_{{\mathsf{o}}{{\mathsf{e}}}}u^{\prime}
(the inequality holds because
n>0
).
∎

Theorem F.7

(Shrinking completeness)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VI.7

theorem

VI.7

Let
d\colon t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}t^{\prime}
be an evaluation with
t^{\prime}
normal.
Then there is a unitary shrinking derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

such that
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.

Proof.

By induction on the length
|d|
of
d
.

If
|d|=0
then
t=t^{\prime}
is
\mathsf{x}
-normal and hence a
strong
fireball (
Proposition

III.1
.
2
).
According to typability of
strong
fireballs (
Lemma

VI.5
), there is unitary shrinking derivation

\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
. Therefore,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
by Lemma
VI.2
.

Otherwise,
|d|>0
and
d
is the concatenation of a first step
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}u
and an evaluation
d^{\prime}\colon u\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}t^{\prime}
, with
|d|=1+|d^{\prime}|
.
By
i.h.
, there is a unitary shrinking derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}

such that
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
By shrinking subject expansion (
Proposition

VI.6
), there is a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
with

•

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+2=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}

if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}\mathsf{m}}u
, since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
;

•

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|t^{\prime}|_{{\mathsf{s}}}+2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}
if
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}{\mathsf{e}}}u
, since
|d|_{\mathsf{m}}=|d^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
∎

Appendix G
Proofs of
Section

VII

Theorem G.1

(Operational and semantic characterizations of normalization)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VII.1

theorem

VII.1

Let
t
be a term. The following are equivalent:

1.

there is a
\mathsf{vsc}
-normal form
u
such that
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}^{*}u
;

2.

t
is
\mathsf{x}
-normalizing;

3.

\llbracket t\rrbracket^{\mathsf{s}}_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
, where
\vec{x}=(x_{1},\dots,x_{n})
is suitable for
t
and

\begin{split}\llbracket t\rrbracket^{\mathsf{s}}_{\vec{x}}\coloneqq\{(({\mathsf{N}}_{1}&,\dots,{\mathsf{N}}_{n}),{\mathsf{M}})\mid\exists\,\Phi\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}\\
&\mbox{ such that ${\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,{\mathsf{N}}_{n}$ are \text{left} and ${\mathsf{M}}$ is \text{right} }\}.\end{split}

Proof.

1)
\Rightarrow
3)

By
Proposition

II.2
,
u
is a
strong
fireball, thus there is a shrinking derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
by
Lemma

VI.5
.
Subject expansion (Prop.
IV.4
.
2
) iterated along
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}^{*}u
gives a shrinking derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright x_{1}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{1},\dots,x_{n}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{n}\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and so
\llbracket t\rrbracket^{\mathsf{s}}_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
.

3)
\Rightarrow
2)

Since
\llbracket t\rrbracket^{\mathsf{s}}_{\vec{x}}\neq\emptyset
, there is a shrinking derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright t
, so
t\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}s
with
s

\mathsf{vsc}
-normal (and hence
\mathsf{x}
-normal, as
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}\,\subseteq\,\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}
) by shrinking correctness (
Theorem

VI.4
).

2)
\Rightarrow
1)

Trivial, as
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}\,\subseteq\,\rightarrow_{\mathsf{vsc}}
and by
Proposition

III.1
.
2
.
∎

Appendix H
Proofs of
Section

VIII

The following proposition has a stronger and more involved statement with respect to the one in the body of the paper in order to have the right
i.h.
to prove it.

Proposition H.1

(Types bound the size of derivations for normal forms)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.1

proposition

VIII.1

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation.

1.

Inert:
if
t
is a
strong
inert term, then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Gamma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|
.

2.

Fireball:
if
t
is a
strong
fireball then

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Gamma}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|
.

Proof.

By mutual induction on the definition of
strong
inert term and
strong
fireball.
Note that the statement for the former is stronger than the one for the latter, hence for
strong
inert terms it is enough to prove Point
1
.
Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

t=x
(which is
strong
inert). So, necessarily, for some finite set of indices
I
,

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hskip 5.55557pt\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 4.02371pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left[\vrule height=16.21385pt,depth=2.5pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 25.15569pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{ax}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{}}\hbox{${x\!:\![{\mathsf{A}}_{i}]}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{A}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right]_{{i\in I}}$}}}\hbox{${x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}}\vdash x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}}\par}}}$}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{A}}_{i}]_{{i\in I}}
and
\Gamma=x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
.
Since
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
and
|{\mathsf{M}}|=|\hat{\Gamma}|
,
then
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\hat{\Gamma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|
.

•

Application
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}
(which is
strong
inert).
Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 5.39755pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.67499pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 30.67537pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
By
i.h.
,
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Delta}|-|[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]|=|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|-1
and
|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Sigma}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|
.
Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|-1+|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|.\end{array}

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]
(which is
strong
inert).
Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 16.03297pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.41142pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 27.14412pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.51772pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
By
i.h.
,
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|
and
|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|
.
So,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|-|{\mathsf{M}}|.\end{array}

•

Explicit substitution on fireball
,
i.e.

t=f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]
. Then necessarily

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.29654pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 17.41142pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 28.40768pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta,x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Sigma\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${\Delta\uplus\Sigma}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

where
\Gamma=\Delta\uplus\Sigma
.
By
i.h.
,
|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|
and
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|
.
Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|&=&|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|\hat{\Sigma}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|.\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|.\end{array}

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

t=\lambda x.{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
.

\Phi
is necessarily of the form (for some set of indices
I
)

\mathsf{many}

\left[\vrule height=36.04951pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.
\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.66548pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.5941pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi^{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right]_{{i\in I}}
\uplus_{{i\in I}}\Gamma_{i}\vdash\lambda x.f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]_{{i\in I}}

where
{\mathsf{M}}=\biguplus_{{i\in I}}[{\mathsf{O}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}_{i}]
,
\Gamma=\biguplus_{{i\in I}}\Gamma_{i}
.
By
i.h.
,
|\Phi^{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|\hat{\Gamma_{i}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{N}}_{i}|
.
Therefore,

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&\sum_{{i\in I}}(|\Phi^{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1)\\
&\leq_{\textit{i.h.}}&\sum_{{i\in I}}(|\hat{\Gamma_{i}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{N}}_{i}|+1)\\
&=&\sum_{{i\in I}}|\hat{\Gamma_{i}}|+\sum_{{i\in I}}(|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{N}}_{i}|+1)\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|+\sum_{{i\in I}}|{\mathsf{N}}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|+|{\mathsf{M}}|.\end{array}

∎

Corollary H.2

(Types bound the size of
strong
fireballs)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.2

corollary

VIII.2

Let
t
be a
strong
fireball and
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a shrinking derivation. Then
|t|\leq|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Gamma}|
.

Proof.

By
Lemma

VI.2
,
|t|\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
, and by

Proposition

VIII.1
.
2

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Gamma}|
.
∎

Theorem H.3

(Lax bounds of kind 3)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.4

theorem

VIII.4

Let
t
and
u
be closed normalizing terms. Then
tu
normalizes if and only if
U(t,u)\neq\emptyset
. Moreover, if
t
and
u
are normal,
d:tu\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}s
, and
s
is normal then
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|\leq|{\mathsf{M}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+1
for every composable pair
({\mathsf{M}},{\mathsf{N}})\in U(t,u)
.

Proof.

If
tu
normalizes then by shrinking completeness (Thm.
VI.7
) there exists a shrinking
derivation
\Phi
for
tu
. The last rule of
\Phi
is necessarily
@
, and the premises of that rule
provide a composable pair in
U(t,u)
. Vice versa, if
U(t,u)\neq\emptyset
then every composable pair
induces a shrinking derivation for
tu
, which is then typable. By shrinking correctness (Thm.
VI.4
),
tu
is
\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}
-normalizing.

Now, the
moreover
part. Let
({\mathsf{M}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}},{\mathsf{M}})\in U(t,u)
be a composable pair. Then there are two derivations
\Phi_{t}\vartriangleright\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}
and
\Phi_{u}\vartriangleright\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
. We compose them via a
@
rule into a derivation
\Phi
for
tu
:

\Phi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hskip 6.52779pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 12.79285pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 23.61116pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{t}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\vdash t\!:\![{\mathsf{M}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{u}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{${}\vdash tu\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

Note that the definition of composable pair guarantees that
{\mathsf{N}}
is right shrinking, so that
\Phi
is shrinking.
By shrinking correctness (Thm.
VI.4
),
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Phi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
. Now, since for normal terms types bound the size of the derivation (Prop.
VIII.1
), we obtain
|\Phi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|[{\mathsf{M}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]|
and
|\Phi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|{\mathsf{M}}|
. Therefore,
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}\leq|[{\mathsf{M}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]|+|{\mathsf{M}}|+1
, as required. ∎

Lemma H.4

(
\sim
-Invariants)
.
†
†
margin:

Proof p.
VIII.6

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
be two derivations such
that
\Phi\sim\Psi
. Then
|\Phi|=|\Psi|
,
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
,

\#{\mathsf{M}}=\#{\mathsf{N}}
,
\mathsf{dom}(\Gamma)=\mathsf{dom}(\Delta)
, and
\#(\Gamma(x))=\#(\Delta(x))

for every variable
x
. Moreover,
\Phi
is shrinking (resp. unitary shrinking) if and only if
\Psi
is.

Proof.

By straightforward induction on the derivation
\Phi
.
∎

The following proposition has a stronger and more involved statement with respect to the one in the body of the paper in order to have the right
i.h.
to prove it.

Proposition H.5

(Size representation)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.7

proposition

VIII.7

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation.

1.

Inert:
if
t
is a
strong
inert term, then for every multi type
{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\#{\mathsf{N}}=\#{\mathsf{M}}
there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\Psi\sim\Phi
and

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|
.

2.

Fireball:
if
t
is a
strong
fireball, then there is a derivation

\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\Psi\sim\Phi
and

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|
.

Proof.

By mutual induction on the definition of
strong
inert term and
strong
fireball. Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

t=x
. Then
\Phi
is a
\mathsf{many}
rule with
n
premises
\Phi_{i}
for

i\in\{1,\ldots n\}
, which are all axioms, that is,
|\Phi_{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
. Therefore
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
. If

{\mathsf{N}}=[{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{n}]
then the derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Delta\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
for
the statement is made out of
n
axioms of types
{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{n}
over a
\mathsf{many}
rule and
\Delta

is simply
{\mathsf{N}}
, so that
|\hat{\Delta}|=|{\mathsf{N}}|
. Then
\Phi\sim\Psi
and

|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|
.

•

Inert application
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}
. Then
\Phi
ends with rule
@
. Let

\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
be its two premises. By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
such that

\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|
.
Let
{\mathsf{N}}
be a multi type such that
\#{\mathsf{N}}=\#{\mathsf{M}}
. Note that rule
@
forces the multi
type assigned by
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
i_{\mathsf{s}}
to be a singleton. By
i.h.
, there is a derivation

\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]
such that

\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|[{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]|=|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-1
.
We have the following derivation
\Psi
:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.5761pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.78094pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.46503pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}\multimap{\mathsf{N}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Clearly,
\Phi\sim\Psi
. Let
\Delta\coloneqq\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\small\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|-1+|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\end{array}

For point 2, we use point 1 with
{\mathsf{N}}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{G}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}]
, for which

|{\mathsf{N}}|=0
, so that equation
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|
also
holds true.

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]
.
Then
\Phi
ends with rule
\mathsf{es}
. Let
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
be its two premises and Let

{\mathsf{N}}
be a multi type such that
\#{\mathsf{N}}=\#{\mathsf{M}}
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and

|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|=|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|
. By
Lemma
VIII.6
,
{\mathsf{O}}
has the same cardinality of the type
{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}
given by

\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
x
, which is also the same type given by
\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}
. We can then apply
the
i.h.
and obtain a derivation
\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
such that

\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|
.
We have the following derivation
\Psi
:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.21152pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.73409pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 29.32263pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.51772pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Clearly,
\Phi\sim\Psi
. Let
\Delta\coloneqq\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\small\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|+|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|+|\hat{\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|-|{\mathsf{N}}|\end{array}

For point 2, we use point 1 with
{\mathsf{N}}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{G}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}]
, for which

|{\mathsf{N}}|=0
, so that equation
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|
also
holds true.

•

Abstraction
,
i.e.

f_{\mathsf{s}}=\lambda x.{f^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
.
Then
\Phi
ends with a rule
\mathsf{many}
with
n
premises, each one ending with a
\lambda
rule. Let

\Phi^{i}
be the premise of the
\lambda
rule of the
i
-th derivation above the
\mathsf{many}
rule, for

i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}\eqqcolon I
.
By
i.h.
, there exists a derivation
\Psi^{i}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}^{\prime}\vdash f^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}
such that
\Psi^{i}\sim\Phi^{i}
and
|\Phi^{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{i}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}^{\prime}|
.
We have the following derivation:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 13.77861pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\lower 0.0pt\hbox{\hbox{$\left[\vrule height=36.04951pt,depth=1.94444pt,width=0.0pt\right.$}$\left.\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.36113pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\lambda$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 32.84602pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to18.5941pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi^{i}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}^{\prime}\vdash f^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i}\vdash\lambda x.f^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}$}}\par}\par}}}$}\right]_{{i\in I}}$}}}\hbox{$\uplus_{{i\in I}}\Delta_{i}\vdash\lambda x.f^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]_{{i\in I}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Clearly,
\Phi\sim\Psi
. Let
\Delta\coloneqq\uplus_{{i\in I}}\Delta_{i}
and
{\mathsf{N}}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]_{{i\in I}}
. We have:

\small\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&n+\sum_{{i\in I}}|\Phi^{i}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&n+\sum_{{i\in I}}(|\hat{\Delta_{i}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}^{\prime}|)\\
&=&\sum_{{i\in I}}|\hat{\Delta_{i}}|+(n+\sum_{{i\in I}}(|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}|+|{\mathsf{O}}_{i}^{\prime}|))\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|[{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}_{i}\multimap{\mathsf{O}}_{i}]_{{i\in I}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|\end{array}

•

Explicit substitution on fireball
,
i.e.

t=f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]
.
Then
\Phi
ends with rule
\mathsf{es}
. Let
\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
be its two premises.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and

|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|
. By
Lemma
VIII.6
,
{\mathsf{O}}
has the same cardinality of the type
{\mathsf{O}}^{\prime}
given by

\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
x
, which is also the same type given by
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
i_{\mathsf{s}}
.
By
i.h.
, there is a derivation
\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
such that

\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|
.
We have the following derivation
\Psi
:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 20.40248pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 19.58998pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.5136pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Clearly,
\Phi\sim\Psi
. Let
\Delta\coloneqq\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{O}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|\hat{\Delta_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Delta}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|\end{array}

∎

Theorem H.6

(Weak exact bounds of kind 3)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.8

theorem

VIII.8

Let
t
and
u
be normal.
If
d:tu\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}s
and
s
is normal then there
exist
{\mathsf{M}}\in\llbracket t\rrbracket
and
{\mathsf{N}}\in\llbracket u\rrbracket
such that
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|{\mathsf{M}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+1
.

Proof.

By shrinking completeness (
Theorem

VI.7
), there is a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright tu
with
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
The last rule of
\Psi
is an
@
rule between two derivations

\Psi_{t}
and
\Psi_{u}
. Note that
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\Psi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1
.
According to size representation (
Proposition

VIII.7
.
2
) and
\sim
-invariants (
Lemma

VIII.6
), there are derivations
\Psi_{t}^{\prime}\vartriangleright\,\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
and
\Psi_{u}^{\prime}\vartriangleright\,\vdash u\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}
such that
|\Psi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|{\mathsf{M}}|
and
|\Psi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|{\mathsf{N}}|
, that is,
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Psi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Psi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1=|{\mathsf{M}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}|+1
with
{\mathsf{M}}\in\llbracket t\rrbracket
and
{\mathsf{N}}\in\llbracket u\rrbracket
. ∎

The next lemma shows that every derivation for a
strong
fireball admits a size dissection. For the induction to go
through, we need to reinforce the disjoint names requirement and to have a stronger statement for inert terms, including
a different size requirement.
Terminology
: a substitution
\sigma

extends
a substitution
\tau
if

\mathsf{dom}(\sigma)\supseteq\mathsf{dom}(\tau)
and
\sigma(x)=\tau(x)
for every
x\in\mathsf{dom}(\tau)
. We also use
{\sf gt}({\mathsf{T}})
,
{\sf gt}({\mathsf{M}})
,
{\sf gt}(\Gamma)
, and
{\sf gt}(\Phi)
for
the set of ground types occurring in
{\mathsf{T}}
,
{\mathsf{M}}
,
\Gamma
, and
\Phi
.

Lemma H.7

(Size dissection)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.10

lemma

VIII.10

Let
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
be a derivation and
S
be a set of ground types containing

{\sf gt}(\Phi)
.

1.

Inert:
if
t
is a
strong
inert term, then for every multi type
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
and for every substitution
\tau

such that
\mathsf{dom}(\tau)={\sf gt}({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})
and
\tau({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})={\mathsf{M}}
there exist a substitution
\sigma
extending
\tau

and a derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
forming a dissection of
\Phi
such that

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|_{\mathsf{m}}-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|_{\mathsf{m}}
and
{\sf gt}(\Psi)\cap S=\emptyset
.

2.

Fireball:
if
t
is a
strong
fireball, then there exist a substitution
\sigma
and a derivation

\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
forming a size dissection of
\Phi
such that
{\sf gt}(\Psi)\cap S=\emptyset
.

Proof.

By mutual induction on the definition of
strong
inert term and
strong
fireball. Cases:

•

Variable
,
i.e.

t=x
. Then
\Phi
has the following shape:

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 28.16698pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\left[x\!:\![{\mathsf{T}}_{i}]\vdash^{\infty}x\!:\!{\mathsf{T}}_{i}\right]_{i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}}$}}\hbox{$x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}\vdash^{\infty}x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{n}]
and
\Gamma
being simply
x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
. Note that

|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0
. Take
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{n}}]
with
{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{n}}
fresh variables with respect to
S
(thus in particular not appearing in
{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{n}
). Then the derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
for the statement is:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{many}$}}}\hbox{\hskip 25.22253pt\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$\left[x\!:\![{\mathsf{G}}_{i}]\vdash^{\infty}x\!:\!{\mathsf{G}}_{i}\right]_{i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}}$}}\hbox{$x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma^{\prime}
being simply
x\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
. The substitution is
\sigma\coloneqq\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{1}\}\ldots\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{n}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{n}\}
.

–

Skeleton
: clearly,
\Phi\sim\Psi
.

–

Representation
: clearly,
\sigma({\mathsf{N}})={\mathsf{M}}
, that implies
\sigma(\Psi)=\Phi
.

–

Reinforced disjoint names
: by construction the ground types in
{\sf gt}(\Psi)=\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{n}}\}
are not in
S
.

–

Types size
:
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|

For point 2, simply note that
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=0=|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|+|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|
.

•

Inert application
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}
. Then
\Phi
ends with rule
@
:

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.50662pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 23.44766pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 33.96509pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

with
\Gamma=\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
.
By
i.h.
(point 2) with respect to
S^{\prime}\coloneqq S\cup\mathsf{dom}(\tau)
, there exist a substitution

\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and derivation
\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}
such that they
form a size dissection of
\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
such that
{\sf gt}(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S^{\prime}=\emptyset
.

Consider now the type
[{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]
. Because of the properties given by the
i.h.
above, we
have that
\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}(\tau([{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]))=[\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime})\multimap\tau({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})]=[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
. Let
\tau^{\prime}
be

\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\circ\tau|_{{\sf gt}([{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}])}
, that is the restriction of

\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\circ\tau
to the ground types in
[{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]
. By
i.h.
(point 1) with respect
to
S^{\prime\prime}\coloneqq S\cup(\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\setminus{\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}))
, there exist a
substitution
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
extending
\tau^{\prime}
and a derivation

\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]
forming a dissection of

\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
such that
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|[{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]|=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|-1
and
{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S^{\prime\prime}=\emptyset
.
We have the following derivation
\Psi
:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 7.50662pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$@$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 24.85045pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 36.77065pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Let
\sigma\coloneqq\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\circ\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
. We have to show that
(\Psi,\sigma)
is a
dissection satisfying the statement of point 1.

–

Skeleton
: the two
i.h.
give
\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
, that imply
\Phi\sim\Psi
.

–

Representation
: by choice of
S^{\prime}
and
S^{\prime\prime}
,
\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})={\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime})
, on which they agree because
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
extends
\tau^{\prime}
, that is,

\sigma=\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
on
\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})
and similarly for
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\sigma(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})=\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})=_{\textit{i.h.}}\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
and similarly

\sigma(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})=\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
, that is,
\sigma(\Psi)=\Phi
.

–

Reinforced disjoint names
: we have that
{\sf gt}(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S^{\prime}=\emptyset
and

{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S^{\prime\prime}=\emptyset
. Since both
S^{\prime}
and
S^{\prime\prime}
contain

S
, we have that the reinforced disjoint names requirement holds:
{\sf gt}(\Psi)\cap S=({\sf gt}(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cup{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}))\cap S=({\sf gt}(\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S)\cup({\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S)=\emptyset\cup\emptyset=\emptyset
.

–

Types size
: let
\Gamma^{\prime}\coloneqq\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\small\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+1\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|+1\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|-1+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|+1\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma}|^{\prime}-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|.\end{array}

For point 2, if
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}]
we use point 1 with
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}]
where
{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}
are
not in
{\sf gt}(\Phi)
, and
\tau\coloneqq\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{1}\}\ldots\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}\}
—clearly
\tau
is a substitution such that
\tau({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})={\mathsf{M}}
and
\mathsf{dom}(\tau)={\sf gt}({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})
. Note that
|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|=0
, so that equation
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|
also holds true.

•

Explicit substitution on inert
,
i.e.

t=i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]
.
Then
\Phi
ends with rule
\mathsf{es}
:

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.14206pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.40082pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 31.8227pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.51772pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Let
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
and
\tau
be such that
\tau({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})={\mathsf{M}}
and
\mathsf{dom}(\tau)={\sf gt}({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})
.
By
i.h.
(point 1), there exist a substitution
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
extending
\tau
and a derivation

\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
forming a dissection of

\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
such that
|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|
and
{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S=\emptyset
.

Let
\sigma_{{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}}
be the restriction of
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
to
{\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime})
. By
i.h.
(point 1) with respect
to
S^{\prime}\coloneqq S\cup(\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\setminus{\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}))
, there exist a substitution

\sigma_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
extending
\sigma_{{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}}
and a derivation

\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vartriangleright\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}
forming a dissection of

\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
such that
|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|
.
We have the following derivation
\Psi
:

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 18.14204pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.80359pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 34.62825pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.51772pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

–

Skeleton
: the two
i.h.
give
\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and
\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
, that imply
\Phi\sim\Psi
.

–

Representation
: by choice of
S^{\prime}
,
\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}})={\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime})
, on which they agree because
\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
extends
\sigma_{{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}}
, that is,
\sigma=\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
on
\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})
and similarly for
\sigma_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then
\sigma(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})=\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})=_{\textit{i.h.}}\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
and similarly
\sigma(\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}})=\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
, that is,
\sigma(\Psi)=\Phi
.

–

Reinforced disjoint names
: we have that
{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S=\emptyset
and

{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S^{\prime}=\emptyset
. Since
S^{\prime}
contains
S
, we have that
the reinforced disjoint names requirement holds:
{\sf gt}(\Psi)\cap S=({\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cup{\sf gt}(\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}))\cap S=({\sf gt}(\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S)\cup({\sf gt}(\Psi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap S)=\emptyset\cup\emptyset=\emptyset
.

–

Types size
: let
\Gamma^{\prime}\coloneqq\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}
. Then

\small\begin{array}[]{rcl}|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}&=&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\Phi_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}|_{\mathsf{m}}+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|\\
&=_{\textit{i.h.}}&|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i^{\prime}_{\mathsf{s}}}}|+|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|\\
&=&|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|-|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|\end{array}

For point 2, if
{\mathsf{M}}=[{\mathsf{T}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{T}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}]
we use point 1 with
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}\coloneqq[{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}]
where
{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}},\ldots,{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}
are
not in
{\sf gt}(\Phi)
, and
\tau\coloneqq\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{1}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{1}\}\ldots\{{\mathsf{G}}_{i_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}}{\shortleftarrow}{\mathsf{T}}_{\#{\mathsf{M}}}\}
—clearly
\tau
is a substitution such that
\tau({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})={\mathsf{M}}
and
\mathsf{dom}(\tau)={\sf gt}({\mathsf{M}}^{\prime})
. Note that
|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|=0
, so that equation
|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|\hat{\Gamma^{\prime}}|+|{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}|
also holds true.

,

Skeleton

\mathsf{es}

\Phi=\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 20.33301pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 21.2567pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 34.01366pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Phi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

\Gamma\coloneqq\Gamma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}
.

\Psi\coloneqq\hbox{$\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 20.33302pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\lower 3.21385pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\hrule height=0.4pt\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hskip 3.00003pt\hbox{\lower 2.15277pt\hbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{es}$}}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{\hbox{}\hskip 15.00002pt\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 22.65948pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\hskip 36.81921pt\hbox{}}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{}\vbox{\hbox{\lower 1.50693pt\vbox{\vskip 3.01385pt\par}}\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to20.02766pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}},x\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$$}}\hbox{\hbox{}\vbox to19.66656pt{\leaders{\vbox to3.44444pt{\vss\hbox{.}\vss\par}}{\vfill}\par}\hbox to0.0pt{ \vbox{\vskip 5.16663pt\hbox{\ignorespaces$\Psi_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}$}\vskip 5.16663pt\par}\hss}}}\par}\par}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}i_{\mathsf{s}}\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}}\hbox{$\Gamma^{\prime}_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\uplus\Gamma^{\prime}_{i_{\mathsf{s}}}\vdash^{\infty}f_{\mathsf{s}}[x{\shortleftarrow}i_{\mathsf{s}}]\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}$}}\par}\par}}}$}

Skeleton

i.h.
give
\Phi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}\sim\Psi_{f_{\mathsf{s}}}

\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{f_{\mathsf{s}}})\cap\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{i_{\mathsf{s}}})={\sf gt}({\mathsf{N}}^{\prime})

Theorem H.8

(Exact bounds of kind 3)
.
†
†
margin:

See p.
VIII.11

theorem

VIII.11

Let
t
and
u
be closed
strong
fireballs. If
d:tu\rightarrow_{\mathsf{x}}^{*}s
and
s
is normal
then
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=\inf\{|{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1\ |\ \exists\sigma\mbox{ such that }(\sigma({\mathsf{N}})\multimap\sigma({\mathsf{M}}),\sigma({\mathsf{O}}))\in U(t,u)\}
.

Proof.

We show a construction that from any shrinking derivation for
s
gives types
{\mathsf{M}}
,
{\mathsf{N}}
, and

{\mathsf{O}}
as in the set of the statement and such that
|{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1\geq 2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}
. The
inf
of that set is then obtained by taking a minimal shrinking derivation,
that is a unitary shrinking one, for which then the inequality is an equality.

By shrinking completeness (
Theorem

VI.7
), there exists a derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\vdash tu\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
such that
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}\leq|\Phi|_{\mathsf{m}}
. The
last rule of
\Phi
is an
@
rule between two derivations

\Phi_{t}\vartriangleright\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]
and

\Phi_{u}\vartriangleright\vdash^{\infty}u\!:\!{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}
, for some
{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}
. Note that
|\Phi|=|\Phi_{t}|+|\Phi_{u}|+1
. By size
dissection (Lemma
VIII.10
) applied to
\Phi_{t}
(with respect to
S\coloneqq{\sf gt}(\Phi)
), there exist a substitution
\sigma_{t}
and a derivation

\Theta_{t}\vartriangleright\vdash^{\infty}t\!:\![{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]
forming a size dissection of
\Phi_{t}
. In
particular, we have
\sigma_{t}([{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}])=[{\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}]
and

|\Phi_{t}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]|
. By size dissection
(
Lemma

VIII.10
) applied to
\Phi_{u}
(with respect to
S^{\prime}\coloneqq{\sf gt}(\Phi)\cup\mathsf{dom}(\sigma_{t})
) there exist a substitution
\sigma_{u}
and a derivation

\Theta_{u}\vartriangleright\vdash^{\infty}u\!:\!{\mathsf{O}}
forming a size dissection of
\Phi_{u}
. In particular,
we have
\sigma_{u}({\mathsf{O}})={\mathsf{N}}^{\prime}
and
|\Phi_{u}|_{\mathsf{m}}=|{\mathsf{O}}|
. Note that

\sigma_{t}([{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}])
and
\sigma_{u}({\mathsf{O}})
form a composable pair because
{\mathsf{M}}^{\prime}
is right (because
\Phi
is shrinking). By construction,
the domains of
\sigma_{t}
and
\sigma_{u}
are disjoint, so that we can define
\sigma
as
\sigma_{t}\cup\sigma_{u}
. Also,

|\Phi|=|\Phi_{t}|+|\Phi_{u}|+1=|[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1
, that is,
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}\leq|\Phi|=|[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1
. Now, by starting with a unitary shrinking
derivation
\Phi
for
tu
, by correctness,

2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Phi|
, that is,
2|d|_{\mathsf{m}}+|s|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|[{\mathsf{N}}\multimap{\mathsf{M}}]|+|{\mathsf{O}}|+1
.
∎

We conclude with the proof of a corollary of the size representation property (
Proposition

VIII.7
.
2
), and not of the previous theorem.

Corollary H.9

(Minimal types catch the size of normal forms)

margin:

See p.
VIII.12

corollary

VIII.12

Let
t
be a
strong
fireball. There exists a unitary shrinking derivation
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such
that
|t|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Gamma}|
.

Proof.

By Prop.
VI.5
.
2
, there exists a unitary shrinking derivation
\Psi\vartriangleright t
.
By
Lemma

VI.2
,
|t|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}
.
By size representation (
Proposition

VIII.7
.
2
), there
exists
\Phi\vartriangleright\Gamma\vdash t\!:\!{\mathsf{M}}
such that
\Phi\sim\Psi
and
|\Psi|_{\mathsf{m}}=|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Gamma}|
(thus
|t|_{{\mathsf{s}}}=|{\mathsf{M}}|+|\hat{\Gamma}|
).
According to
\sim
-invariants (
Lemma

VIII.6
),
\Phi
is unitary shrinking.
∎
